wow-viewer/src/viewer/WoWViewer/Workbench/Services/MlTraining/MlTrainingService.cs	000459	ImGui.TextWrapped	Launch the multichannel V7.1 terrain trainer from the viewer, watch stdout/stderr, and monitor live loss curves from training_log.json. V7.1 uses minimap, normals, WDL height priors, per-tile bounds hints, liquid masks, and object-footprint masks because minimap pixels alone do not carry enough height information to reconstruct valid terrain. WDL is the critical fallback seam: for many maps we have minimaps and WDL even when full ADT-derived heightmaps are missing, so the model must preserve that low-resolution terrain correlation instead of pretending minimap-only reconstruction is sufficient. Alpha-mask and tileset decomposition should be treated as a separate minimap-to-texture-layer model, not as the core terrain-height job.	0009	text	literal	-

wow-viewer/src/viewer/WoWViewer/Workbench/Services/Pm4Workbench/Pm4WorkbenchService.cs	000125	ImGui.CollapsingHeader	PM4 Glossary / Evidence	0014	label	literal	-
wow-viewer/src/viewer/WoWViewer/Workbench/Services/Pm4Workbench/Pm4WorkbenchService.cs	000128	ImGui.TextWrapped	PM4/PD4 are server-side navigation-mesh files: walkable surfaces, their adjacency, and what blocks movement. The cyan (MSCN) and magenta (MSPV) cubes are the visible projection of this data. The single authoritative reading is docs/research/pm4-format-reference.md.	0009	text	literal	-
wow-viewer/src/viewer/WoWViewer/Workbench/Services/Pm4Workbench/Pm4WorkbenchService.cs	000129	ImGui.BulletText	MSUR — 32-byte surface record. 0x01 = MSVI index count; 0x02 = MSLK window count; 0x04/0x08/0x0C = normal; 0x10 = plane distance; 0x14 = MSVI window start; 0x18 = MSLK window start; 0x1C = IEEE-754 float, the producing placement's Z.	0009	text	literal	-
wow-viewer/src/viewer/WoWViewer/Workbench/Services/Pm4Workbench/Pm4WorkbenchService.cs	000130	ImGui.BulletText	MSLK — the surface-adjacency graph. msur_index names a neighbouring surface in the tile addressed by 0x0C. 0x01 is a zero-based source-edge ordinal. 0x04 is the object-group id and equals MPRL.0x04. A non-empty MSPI window is a vertical wall quad; an empty one is open passage.	0009	text	literal	-
wow-viewer/src/viewer/WoWViewer/Workbench/Services/Pm4Workbench/Pm4WorkbenchService.cs	000131	ImGui.BulletText	MSCN — ordered connector/boundary node positions. Objects share nodes; nothing in the file indexes MSCN. Visible as the cyan cubes.	0009	text	literal	-
wow-viewer/src/viewer/WoWViewer/Workbench/Services/Pm4Workbench/Pm4WorkbenchService.cs	000132	ImGui.BulletText	MSPV / MSPI — wall-quad vertices and indices on the blocked subset of MSLK links. Visible as the magenta cubes.	0009	text	literal	-
wow-viewer/src/viewer/WoWViewer/Workbench/Services/Pm4Workbench/Pm4WorkbenchService.cs	000133	ImGui.BulletText	MSVI / MSVT — mesh indices and vertex positions. A surface is a polygon fan over MSVI[MSUR.0x14 .. + MSUR.0x01) into MSVT.	0009	text	literal	-
wow-viewer/src/viewer/WoWViewer/Workbench/Services/Pm4Workbench/Pm4WorkbenchService.cs	000134	ImGui.BulletText	MPRL — terrain-contact points. 0x00 is a per-tile region value; 0x04 is the object-group id; position is the only permuted chunk (height is Y).	0009	text	literal	-
wow-viewer/src/viewer/WoWViewer/Workbench/Services/Pm4Workbench/Pm4WorkbenchService.cs	000135	ImGui.BulletText	MSHD — 32-byte header. 0x00/0x08 are clamped world-unit spans; 0x04 == 1 marks a tile with no surfaces; 0x0C–0x1C are zero.	0009	text	literal	-
wow-viewer/src/viewer/WoWViewer/Workbench/Services/Pm4Workbench/Pm4WorkbenchService.cs	000136	ImGui.BulletText	part / ObjectPartId — viewer-generated split id from the current overlay build, not a raw PM4 field.	0009	text	literal	-
wow-viewer/src/viewer/WoWViewer/Workbench/Services/Pm4Workbench/Pm4WorkbenchService.cs	000137	ImGui.BulletText	MSLK Group — dominant MSLK.0x04 in the current viewer object. A grouping hint, not proof of identity.	0009	text	literal	-
wow-viewer/src/viewer/WoWViewer/Workbench/Services/Pm4Workbench/Pm4WorkbenchService.cs	000138	ImGui.BulletText	PM4 Graph — the viewer's decomposition of the selected object, not a literal raw node graph stored in PM4.	0009	text	literal	-
